# How this repository was made (Río Negro)

This file is written by the build on 2026-10-30, the day the source data was retrieved.

## Sources

| Source | Address | Retrieved | Licence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Legislatura de la Provincia de Río Negro (web.legisrn.gov.ar) | <https://web.legisrn.gov.ar/> | 2026-10-30 | Official legal texts of the province, published by its government for public consultation. Argentina's copyright law (Ley 11.723) has no express exclusion for them; laws are public acts of government, binding on everyone once officially published (Código Civil y Comercial, art. 5). The source states no restriction on reuse. Reproduced with attribution, following the official text. |

Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (America/Argentina/Salta) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Código Civil y Comercial de la Nación, art. 5: "Las leyes rigen después del octavo día de su publicación oficial, o desde el día que ellas determinen", counted under art. 6 (the day of publication is excluded), so a law without its own vigencia clause is in force from the ninth day after its publication in the provincial Boletín Oficial (before 1 August 2015, Código Civil art. 2 as replaced by Ley 16.504, to the same effect). A law's own vigencia clause overrides the rule. https://servicios.infoleg.gob.ar/infolegInternet/anexos/235000-239999/235975/texact.htm

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `36161afd2ec2122a7b453c944b75e6a492044c3f33674476a57c1284e7cf1dc5` (10860905 bytes).
- Builder blob hashes (`git hash-object`): `build.py` `a4faa25af85facb64b956b372f6b528aafdd7ec1`, `check.py` `3d00541d258745461ab52b1b0cb4e1716a27a95d`, `on.py` `644a6c0857fbcc72562032b58d57aa113fee0641`.
- Python 3.12.10; git version 2.46.0.windows.1.

## Checks

1739 versions of 1739 acts read back from git on the day each took effect and on the day before: 0 mismatches. Trailers: 0 commits whose trailers git cannot parse. Health: 0 merge commits on main, 0 commit dates that go backwards, 0 commits dated after the build.

No second build of this corpus has been compared yet.

## Decisions made by hand

- Constitution: the Legislatura's digital edition of the Constitución de la Provincia de Río Negro (sanctioned in Viedma on 3 June 1988; Normas Complementarias art. 24: "Esta Constitución regirá a partir del día cuatro de junio de mil novecientos ochenta y ocho"), shown as enacted, dated 4 June 1988.
- Units: the heading (encabezado: the edition's title page, preface and index, the preamble and everything before Artículo 1) and one file per article. The 26 articles of the Normas Complementarias, numbered again from 1, are the files art-NNN-dup2. Each file holds the article and everything printed after it up to the next article (section headings, page numbers, the Convención's closing note). Nothing is dropped. One paragraph per line, spacing normalised.
- Laws: only laws whose Legislatura record says Normal (Vigente) and lists no later norm under "Se encuentra afectada por", each as its Texto Promulgado (the text as promulgated), dated by the record's Entrada en vigencia; newest first, in parts of about 50.

## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- A model (Claude) wrote the fetch and parse code and chose which norms to include; it wrote no law text. Every word of law comes from the provincial source's own texts.
